- **ion-image-augmentation-intensity-dependent**: Use when training a contrastive encoder on mass spectrometry imaging (MSI) data in ISO mode (isotope ions from the same molecule). - **ion-image-correlation-analysis**: Use when an rMSIproc peak matrix has matrix-related clusters (e.g. silver 'Ag1' annotation) whose S1 spectral and S2 morphological similarity scores fall below the user-specified threshold, so co-localized endogenous ions may be hiding inside the cluster. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-image-correlation-analysis))
- **ion-image-embedding-optimization**: Use when you have 512-dimensional representation vectors output from ResNet18 encoders processing paired augmented ion images, and you need to prevent trivial solutions (representation collapse) during contrastive learning—specifically when optimizing for maximized similarity between augmentations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-image-embedding-optimization) · 2 copies)
- **ion-image-hotspot-truncation**: Use when raw MALDI-MSI pixel-by-ion matrices contain extreme intensity spikes (matrix crystal hotspots) that must be capped before self-supervised ion-image representation learning or any downstream similarity measurement. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-image-hotspot-truncation))
- **ion-image-preprocessing-for-cnn-inference**: Use when ion images (e.g. from METASPACE gold standard GS.tar.gz or the 353 DHB matrix-cluster formulas) must be converted into the fixed-size RGB input expected by a pretrained ResNet-50 CNN for off-sample/on-sample classification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-image-preprocessing-for-cnn-inference))
- **ion-image-quantification-workflow**: Use when when you have imzML mass spectrometry imaging data files and need to convert raw ion image intensities into quantitative lipid abundance (pmol/mm²) using known internal standards. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-image-quantification-workflow) · 3 copies)
- **ion-image-representation-learning**: Use when MSI pixel-by-ion matrices (e.g. [X*Y,P] shape, 198x422 rat brain images) must be converted into dense ion-image representations for colocalized-ion or isotope-ion search without manual annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-image-representation-learning))
- **ion-image-slice-extraction**: Use when an MS imaging dataset (one MassSpectrum object per tissue pixel) must be summarized into a mean spectrum and rendered as spatial ion-image slices at selected m/z values, e.g. reproducing the mouse kidney MALDI imaging Figure 7 layout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/ion-image-slice-extraction))
- **ion-image-spatial-correlation-analysis**: Use when evaluating spectral/spatial fidelity of MSI reconstruction methods (e.g. MEISTER, FT, linear subspace) against a clean ground-truth dataset with known peak centroids. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-image-spatial-correlation-analysis))
- **ion-intensity-spectrum-parsing**: Use when input records are raw mass spectra (m/z / intensity pairs) that must be converted into the ion:intensity single-line format or Spectrum objects before SPLASH generation or similarity work. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-intensity-spectrum-parsing))
- **ion-list-sorting-and-canonicalization**: Use when a raw spectrum (e.g. a MassBank PR100026-style ion list such as '138.0641:71.59 195.0815:261.7') must be converted into the deterministic canonical ion:intensity string that feeds SPLASH hashing, similarity search, or cross-database comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-list-sorting-and-canonicalization))
- **ion-mobility-4d-annotation-workflow**: Use when you have ion-mobility LC-IMS-MS/MS data (e.g. timsTOF / PASEF) and want CCS-aware annotations — 4D feature extraction with collision cross section, CCS calibration and filtering, CCS-aware library matching, and (optional) networking. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/workflows/ion-mobility-4d-annotation) · 2 copies)
- **ion-mobility-adduct-dataset-partitioning**: Use when a CCS prediction model must be built or reproduced per ion species — e.g. splitting McLean CCS Compendium measurements into [M−H]−, [M+H]+ and [M+Na]+ subsets for SVR regression and reporting median relative error and RMSEV. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-adduct-dataset-partitioning))
- **ion-mobility-arrival-time-conversion**: Use when you have a feature table containing raw ion mobility arrival time measurements paired with experimentally assigned biomolecular class labels (e.g., lipid, protein, carbohydrate), and you need to compute CCS values for downstream structural or comparative analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-arrival-time-conversion) · 3 copies)
- **ion-mobility-arrival-time-correction**: Use when working with traveling-wave IMS-MS data (SLIM-based IMS or Waters Synapt) where arrival times from feature files must be adjusted for instrument-specific delays before fitting CCS calibration functions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-arrival-time-correction))
- **ion-mobility-arrival-time-metrics**: Use when raw MS data from LC-IMS-MS acquisitions (or any run with IMS separation) must be quality-controlled by computing arrival-time or 1/K0 errors for auto-tracked or user-specified precursor ions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-arrival-time-metrics))
- **ion-mobility-arrival-time-peak-detection**: Use when you have drift tube IM-MS mzML data, a confirmed retention time for a molecular formula's primary ion, and single-field CCS calibration coefficients (Tfix, Beta) with a known drift gas, and you need IM peaks and DTCCS(N2) values for adduct ion species. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-arrival-time-peak-detection))
- **ion-mobility-bin-index-preservation**: Use when converting profile-mode HDMSE/ion-mobility mass spectrometry data (mzML or Waters UNIFI) to a columnar format and the drift-time dimensions (bin, dt) must survive the conversion for later IMS trace or CCS analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-bin-index-preservation))
- **ion-mobility-calibration-curve-fitting**: Use when converting ion arrival times from single-field RapidFire-DTIMS-MS, SLIM-based IMS, Waters Synapt TWIMS, or Bruker timsTOF into CCS values using a calibrant such as Agilent tune-mix; not needed for stepped-field DTIMS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-calibration-curve-fitting))
- **ion-mobility-calibration-curve-fitting**: Use when you have TWIM-MS experimental data with arrival times and m/z values, and access to calibrant reference standards with known CCS values (typically loaded from a calibration template). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-calibration-curve-fitting) · 2 copies)
- **ion-mobility-calibration-plotting**: Use when calibrating drift/arrival times to collision cross section (CCS) for TWIMS or SLIM IMS-MS data stored in MZA (HDF5) format, and when a fitted-calibration plot is needed for QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-calibration-plotting))
- **ion-mobility-calibration-validation**: Use when when you have positive- or negative-mode ion mobility spectrometry data with tunemix reference standards (known m/z, drift times, and CCS values) and need to verify that the calibration model accurately captures the relationship between drift time, reference m/z, and collision cross. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-calibration-validation) · 3 copies)
- **ion-mobility-ccs-calibration**: Use when you have IMS-MS arrival-time/feature data (e.g. UIMF frame metadata plus Features_csv tables) and need to convert drift or arrival times into calibrated CCS values, choosing among single-field, stepped-field, or traveling-wave calibration methods. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-ccs-calibration))
- **ion-mobility-ccs-experimental-comparison**: Use when per-conformer or Boltzmann-averaged CCS values (e.g., from HPCCS trajectory calculations) need to be validated against TWIMS experimental CCS references, or when ranking conformers to find the one matching the measured CCS. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/ion-mobility-ccs-experimental-comparison))
- **ion-mobility-class-stratification**: Use when you have TWIM-MS experimental data with assigned biomolecular class labels (e.g., peptides, lipids, carbohydrates) and arrival time measurements, and you need to compute CCS values conditioned on class membership. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-class-stratification) · 2 copies)
- **ion-mobility-demultiplexing-algorithm**: Use when you have raw IM-MS data in UIMF or Agilent MassHunter .d format acquired using multiplexed (compressed) ion mobility pulse sequences, and you need to recover conventional (non-multiplexed) IM-MS spectra with resolved mobility and mass dimensions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-demultiplexing-algorithm) · 3 copies)
- **ion-mobility-dimension-detection**: Use when when processing raw mass spectrometry data files of unknown or mixed provenance, and you need to automatically route IMS inputs to their corresponding analysis pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-dimension-detection) · 4 copies)
- **ion-mobility-dimension-interpolation**: Use when when processing raw IM-MS data (Agilent MassHunter . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-dimension-interpolation) · 3 copies)
- **ion-mobility-feature-classification**: Use when you have high-dimensional TWIM-MS data (arrival time and m/z dimensions) from a multi-omic sample and need to associate experimental features with biomolecular classes *before* running peak detection or feature identification pipelines. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/ion-mobility-feature-classification) · 2 copies)
